## Status

Playable as a living toy, not yet as a game: the Anark intro and soundtrack
play; all eight worlds render and their 2,089 object state machines run at
24 Hz; Mendel spawns, walks with his real NERM brain, wakes the world with his
footsteps and reacts to touch. Still missing: the original per-floor camera,
sounds, save pads and the menus. See the journal at the end of the making-of.

play; all eight worlds render and their 2,089 object state machines run at
24 Hz; Mendel spawns, walks with his real NERM brain, wakes the world with his
footsteps and reacts to touch. Still missing: the original per-floor camera,
sounds, save pads and the menus. See the journal at the end of the making-of.

play; all eight worlds render and their 2,089 object state machines run at
24 Hz; Mendel spawns, walks with his real NERM brain, wakes the world with his
footsteps and reacts to touch. Still missing: the original per-floor camera,
sounds, save pads and the menus. See the journal at the end of the making-of.

play; all eight worlds render and their 2,089 object state machines run at
24 Hz; Mendel spawns, walks with his real NERM brain, wakes the world with his
footsteps and reacts to touch. Still missing: the original per-floor camera,
sounds, save pads and the menus. See the journal at the end of the making-of.

play; all eight worlds render and their 2,089 object state machines run at
24 Hz; Mendel spawns, walks with his real NERM brain, wakes the world with his
footsteps and reacts to touch. Still missing: the original per-floor camera,
sounds, save pads and the menus. See the journal at the end of the making-of.

play; all eight worlds render and their 2,089 object state machines run at
24 Hz; Mendel spawns, walks with his real NERM brain, wakes the world with his
footsteps and reacts to touch. Still missing: the original per-floor camera,
sounds, save pads and the menus. See the journal at the end of the making-of.

play; all eight worlds render and their 2,089 object state machines run at
24 Hz; Mendel spawns, walks with his real NERM brain, wakes the world with his
footsteps and reacts to touch. Still missing: the original per-floor camera,
sounds, save pads and the menus. See the journal at the end of the making-of.

play; all eight worlds render and their 2,089 object state machines run at
24 Hz; Mendel spawns, walks with his real NERM brain, wakes the world with his
footsteps and reacts to touch. Still missing: the original per-floor camera,
sounds, save pads and the menus. See the journal at the end of the making-of.

play; all eight worlds render and their 2,089 object state machines run at
24 Hz; Mendel spawns, walks with his real NERM brain, wakes the world with his
footsteps and reacts to touch. Still missing: the original per-floor camera,
sounds, save pads and the menus. See the journal at the end of the making-of.

play; all eight worlds render and their 2,089 object state machines run at
24 Hz; Mendel spawns, walks with his real NERM brain, wakes the world with his
footsteps and reacts to touch. Still missing: the original per-floor camera,
sounds, save pads and the menus. See the journal at the end of the making-of.

play; all eight worlds render and their 2,089 object state machines run at
24 Hz; Mendel spawns, walks with his real NERM brain, wakes the world with his
footsteps and reacts to touch. Still missing: the original per-floor camera,
sounds, save pads and the menus. See the journal at the end of the making-of.

play; all eight worlds render and their 2,089 object state machines run at
24 Hz; Mendel spawns, walks with his real NERM brain, wakes the world with his
footsteps and reacts to touch. Still missing: the original per-floor camera,
sounds, save pads and the menus. See the journal at the end of the making-of.

play; all eight worlds render and their 2,089 object state machines run at
24 Hz; Mendel spawns, walks with his real NERM brain, wakes the world with his
footsteps and reacts to touch. Still missing: the original per-floor camera,
sounds, save pads and the menus. See the journal at the end of the making-of.

play; all eight worlds render and their 2,089 object state machines run at
24 Hz; Mendel spawns, walks with his real NERM brain, wakes the world with his
footsteps and reacts to touch. Still missing: the original per-floor camera,
sounds, save pads and the menus. See the journal at the end of the making-of.

play; all eight worlds render and their 2,089 object state machines run at
24 Hz; Mendel spawns, walks with his real NERM brain, wakes the world with his
footsteps and reacts to touch. Still missing: the original per-floor camera,
sounds, save pads and the menus. See the journal at the end of the making-of.

play; all eight worlds render and their 2,089 object state machines run at
24 Hz; Mendel spawns, walks with his real NERM brain, wakes the world with his
footsteps and reacts to touch. Still missing: the original per-floor camera,
sounds, save pads and the menus. See the journal at the end of the making-of.
